# dense_gemm — shipped starting points

GENERATED by `tools/corpus/build.py`. These are upstream-selected configuration groups and backend-selection counts, not measured winners. A group can contain several shapes; its most frequent value is not a performance recommendation.

`same_across_configs` requires an explicit equal value in every member. `partially_specified` records presence, absence and typed value counts; missing parameters are not inferred defaults. JSON null remains distinct from a missing field or a string.

[Development decisions](decisions.md) · [Machine-readable records](tuned_configs.yaml)

Groups share an exact snapshot and source-file list. **Shared fields** are explicitly present and identical in every listed record; combine them with each row's fields. They are neither inferred defaults nor agreement across unlisted shapes. Full configuration anchors remain stable.
